## Answers to the requested questions

**1. Closest generated CSV** 39 non-reference CSV path(s) are exact champion-stream copies and therefore tied as closest. The closest non-identical prediction stream is D:\gci\gemini\scratch\test_rec_match.csv (Spearman 1, MAE 1.58636097561e-09).

**2. Highest-similarity independently trained candidate** D:\gci\_gci_push_tmp\champion\EXP85_CHAMPION_IMPROVEMENT\experiments\exp07_gbdt_feature_ensemble\runs\exp07_signature_seed42_v2\submission_signature_model.csv (TARGET; Spearman 0.918479452341, Pearson 0.780032513997; EXPERIMENT_REJECTED). **3. Greatest prediction diversity** D:\gci\champion\EXP85_MASTER_RECOVERY\runs\phase3_formula_parity_historical_cache_20261101_000000\anchor74_only.csv (TARGET; Spearman 0.863227465517, MAE 0.113136215216). Diversity is not improvement.

**4. Copies or historical-cache replays** Yes. 39 CSV path(s) reproduce the champion prediction stream exactly; 76 byte-identical file duplicate group(s) and additional aligned-stream duplicates are listed. Other replay/cache labels are path clues, not proven lineage.

**5. Independently trained candidate worth investigating further** No new candidate is recommended on correlation alone. The strongest verified fresh-run candidates are the rejected EXP07/EXP08 outputs; consult their OOF gates rather than rerunning or promoting by similarity.

**6. OOF files not comparable to the test champion** 6 OOF prediction column(s) were identified and excluded; full paths and reasons are in CSV_INVENTORY.csv and ALIGNMENT_VALIDATION.csv.

**7. Evidence of improved predictive performance** No test-label or new leaderboard evidence exists in this audit. The related predeclared OOF replications remain rejected: EXP07 independent seed +0.0006971, EXP08 +0.00019798, EXP10 +0.00079358, and EXP11 -0.01087955 pooled AUC delta. EXP07's first screen (+0.0010171) required independent replication and was not a promotion result. Correlation does not establish improvement.

## Interpretation limitations

- Test data has no labels in this audit. No test AUC, error rate, or improvement can be inferred.
- OOF rows are explicitly separated from test rows; they are not correlated against the champion.
- A high correlation means similar ordering/predictions, not a better model.
- Exact source hashes and identical aligned prediction streams identify copies; other provenance labels remain cautious unless supported by run artifacts.
- Full metrics, alignments, file metadata, duplicates, pairwise candidate correlations and hashes are in the adjacent CSV/JSON artifacts.
